=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)
            
        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n
        
        def dfs(i, l, p)
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)
        
        undefinedLCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a
        
        dfs(0, 0, -1)
        for i, j in trips:
            lca = LCA(i, j)
            path = []
            while i != lca:
                freq[i] += 2
                i = parent[i]
            freq[i] += 1
            while j != lca:
                freq[j] += 1
                j = parent[j]
        
        def dp(i, p)
            res0 = 0
            res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    curr = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]
        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))

```python
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)
            
        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n
        
        def dfs(i, l, p)
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)
        
        undefinedLCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a
        
        dfs(0, 0, -1)
        for i, j in trips:
            lca = LCA(i, j)
            path = []
            while i != lca:
                freq[i] += 2
                i = parent[i]
            freq[i] += 1
            while j != lca:
                freq[j] += 1
                j = parent[j]

            res0 = 0
            res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    curr = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]
        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
```

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

```python
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)
            
        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n
        
        def dfs(i, l, p)
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)
        
        undefinedLCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a
        
        dfs(0, 0, -1)
        for i, j in trips:
            lca = LCA(i, j)
            path = []
            while i != lca:
                freq[i] += 2
                i = parent[i]
            freq[i] += 1
            while j != lca:
                freq[j] += 1
                j = parent[j]
        
        def dp(i, p)
            res0 = 0
            res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    curr = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]
        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's produce fixed code:

```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(u: int, v: int) -> int:
            if level[u] > level[v]:
                u, v = v, u
            d = level[v] - level[u]
            while d:
                v = parent[v]
                d -= 1
            if u == v:
                return u
            while u != v:
                u = parent[u]
                v = parent[v]
            return u

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for u, v in trips:
            w = lca(u, v)
            x, y = u, v
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]
            freq[w] += 1

        def dp(u: int, p: int) -> List[int]:
            not_halved = 0
            halved = price[u] // 2 * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    child = dp(v, u)
                    not_halved += max(child)
                    halved += child[0]
            return [not_halved, halved]

        total = sum(freq[i] * price[i] for i in range(n))
        return total - max(dp(0, -1))
```

```
    a = price[u] * cnt[u]  # not halve
```

Let's verify cost DP:
- freq counts occurrences.
- dp(u,p) returns [cost if u not halved, cost if u halved].
- If u not halved: cost = price[u]*freq[u] + sum over children min(child costs) because child can be halved or not.
- If u halved: cost = (price[u]//2)*freq[u] + sum over children child_not_halved because adjacent child cannot be halved.
- answer = min(dp(0,-1)).

Savings DP final:
```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(u: int, v: int) -> int:
            if level[u] > level[v]:
                u, v = v, u
            d = level[v] - level[u]
            while d:
                v = parent[v]
                d -= 1
            if u == v:
                return u
            while u != v:
                u = parent[u]
                v = parent[v]
            return u

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for u, v in trips:
            w = lca(u, v)
            x, y = u, v
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]
            freq[w] += 1

        def dp(u: int, p: int) -> List[int]:
            not_halved = 0
            halved = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    child = dp(v, u)
                    not_halved += max(child)
                    halved += child[0]
            return [not_halved, halved]

        total = sum(freq[i] * price[i] for i in range(n))
        return total - max(dp(0, -1))
```
This is correct. But note `halved` variable in savings DP is "saving if halved", not "cost if halved". Name could be `halved_saving` for clarity. But code okay.

Cost DP final:
```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(u: int, v: int) -> int:
            if level[u] > level[v]:
                u, v = v, u
            d = level[v] - level[u]
            while d:
                v = parent[v]
                d -= 1
            if u == v:
                return u
            while u != v:
                u = parent[u]
                v = parent[v]
            return u

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for u, v in trips:
            w = lca(u, v)
            x, y = u, v
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]
            freq[w] += 1

        def dp(u: int, p: int) -> List[int]:
            not_halved = price[u] * freq[u]
            halved = price[u] // 2 * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    child = dp(v, u)
                    not_halved += min(child)
                    halved += child[0]
            return [not_halved, halved]

        return min(dp(0, -1))
```

```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(u: int, v: int) -> int:
            if level[u] > level[v]:
                u, v = v, u
            d = level[v] - level[u]
            while d:
                v = parent[v]
                d -= 1
            if u == v:
                return u
            while u != v:
                u = parent[u]
                v = parent[v]
            return u

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for u, v in trips:
            w = lca(u, v)
            x, y = u, v
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]
            freq[w] += 1

        def dp(u: int, p: int) -> List[int]:
            # res0: max saving if u is not halved
            # res1: max saving if u is halved
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    child = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(child)
                    res1 += child[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        total = sum(freq[i] * price[i] for i in range(n))
        return total - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Need check if `res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]` is correct for saving if halved. Yes. But wait, if node is halved, the cost contribution is `price[u] // 2 * freq[u]`, so saving is `price[u] * freq[u] - price[u] // 2 * freq[u]` = `(price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]`. Good.

Need maybe if `price[u] - price[u] // 2` equals `(price[u] + 1) // 2`; for odd 3, 2. Good.

Need maybe if `total = sum(freq[i] * price[i] for i in range(n))`; if no trips, total=0, dp returns [0,0], answer=0. Good.

Need maybe if `dp` returns list and `max(child)` works. Good.

Need maybe if `lca` function uses `level` and `parent`; if `u` or `v` is -1? no.

Need maybe if `dfs` root 0 and parent -1; if n=0, dfs(0) error. Constraints n>=1. Good.

Need maybe if `from typing import List` and `List` in nested dp annotation; okay.

Let's maybe derive the DP one more time to ensure savings formula:
Let `S` be set of halved nodes, no adjacent. Total cost = sum_v freq[v]*price[v] - sum_{v in S} freq[v]*(price[v] - floor(price[v]/2)).
We want minimize cost = total - maximize saving. DP computes max saving over independent set. For each node u:
- If u not in S: saving = sum over children max(saving child in S/not).
- If u in S: saving = freq[u]*(price[u] - floor(price[u]/2)) + sum over children saving child not in S.
This is exactly code. Good.

Let's decide final code exactly:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(u: int, v: int) -> int:
            if level[u] > level[v]:
                u, v = v, u
            d = level[v] - level[u]
            while d:
                v = parent[v]
                d -= 1
            if u == v:
                return u
            while u != v:
                u = parent[u]
                v = parent[v]
            return u

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for u, v in trips:
            w = lca(u, v)
            x, y = u, v
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]
            freq[w] += 1

        def dp(u: int, p: int) -> List[int]:
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    child = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(child)
                    res1 += child[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        total = sum(freq[i] * price[i] for i in range(n))
        return total - max(dp(0, -1))
```

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(u: int, v: int) -> int:
            if level[u] > level[v]:
                u, v = v, u
            d = level[v] - level[u]
            while d:
                v = parent[v]
                d -= 1
            if u == v:
                return u
            while u != v:
                u = parent[u]
                v = parent[v]
            return u

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for u, v in trips:
            w = lca(u, v)
            x, y = u, v
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]
            freq[w] += 1

        def dp(u: int, p: int) -> List[int]:
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    child = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(child)
                    res1 += child[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        total = sum(freq[i] * price[i] for i in range(n))
        return total - max(dp(0, -1))
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(u: int, v: int) -> int:
            if level[u] > level[v]:
                u, v = v, u
            d = level[v] - level[u]
            while d:
                v = parent[v]
                d -= 1
            if u == v:
                return u
            while u != v:
                u = parent[u]
                v = parent[v]
            return u

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for u, v in trips:
            w = lca(u, v)
            x, y = u, v
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]
            freq[w] += 1

        def dp(u: int, p: int) -> List[int]:
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    child = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(child)
                    res1 += child[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        total = sum(freq[i] * price[i] for i in range(n))
        return total - max(dp(0, -1))
